```
public class DivArrayByTwo {
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; ++i) {
            arr[i] = arr[i] / 2;
        }
    }
}
```

```
public class DivArrayByTwo {
    //@  requires arr == null;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  assigns arr[*];
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == \old(arr[i]) / 2;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] == \old(arr[j]) / 2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k; i <= k < arr.length; arr[k] == \old(arr[k]);
        //@ loop_writes arr[*], i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; ++i) {
            arr[i] = arr[i] / 2;
        }
    }
}
```

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```
Note that:
+ You SHOULD generate `ensures` statements for every method, and `maintaining` statements for every loop.

assistant: ```
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
public class Find {
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }
}

```

assistant: ```
public class Find {
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr == null || \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] != elem;
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }
}
```

```
public class TwoSum {
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
```
        //@ maintaining (\forall int k, l; 0 < k < i && k < l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target);  
        //@ decreasing n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= n;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 0 < k < i && k <= j; nums[k] + nums[j] != target);  
            //@ decreasing n - j;

--------------- Mutation Iteration 36 ---------------
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target && \result != null && \result.length == 2 && \result[0] == i && \result[1] == j);  
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, j; 0 < i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] != target || \result == null || \result.length != 2);  
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int k, l; 0 < k <= i && k <= l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target);  
        //@ decreasing n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= n;
            //@ maintaining (\exists int k; 0 - 1 <= k <= i && k < j; nums[k] + nums[j] != target);  
            //@ decreasing n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}

public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 < i < j <= nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target && \result != null || \result.length == 2 || \result[0] == i || \result[1] == j);  
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, j; 0 < i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] != target || \result == null || \result.length != 2);  
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
 
        //@ decreasing n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= n;
 
            //@ decreasing n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }

    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 - 1 <= i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target && \result != null && \result.length == 2 || \result[0] == i && \result[1] == j);  
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, j; 0 < i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] != target || \result == null || \result.length != 2);  
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
 
        //@ decreasing n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= n;
 
            //@ decreasing n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
